{"amberApp":1,"files":{"/README.md":"# Lift\n\nA strength-training log: routines, a live workout screen, an exercise library, history, charts, records, body measurements.\n\n## Screens\n- Workout (src/screens/home.tsx): routines in folders, start empty or from a routine, routine editor\n- Live workout (src/components/live-workout.tsx): full-screen overlay with clock, rest timer, finish summary\n- History (src/screens/history.tsx): month calendar and workout list\n- Exercises (src/screens/exercises.tsx): library (~100 + custom), per-exercise charts, records, history\n- Progress (src/screens/progress.tsx): overview, muscle distribution, records, body measurements\n- Settings (src/screens/settings.tsx): units, rest defaults, RPE, weekly goal, plate calculator\n\n## Data\nAll weights are stored in kg (lengths in cm); the unit setting only changes display. Keys in data.json `values`:\n- settings: { unit: \"kg\"|\"lb\", rest (s), autoRest, showRpe, bar (kg), weeklyGoal }\n- workouts: [{ id, name, start (ISO), dur (s), note?, routine?, exs: [{ id, ex (exercise id), rest, ss? (superset group), note?, sets: [{ id, type: N|W|D|F, w, r, rpe, done }] }] }]\n- routines: [{ id, name, folder (folder id or null), exs }] (same exercise shape; order of the array is the display order)\n- folders: [{ id, name }]\n- customExercises: [{ id, name, muscle, equip, custom: true }] (the built-in library lives in src/lib/data.ts)\n- measurements: [{ id, date (yyyy-MM-dd), type: weight|fat|waist|chest|arm|thigh, value }]\n- activeWorkout: the workout in progress, or null (with `rest: { end, total, label }` while the rest timer runs)\n- seeded: true once the 9-week push/pull/legs demo data has been written\n\n## How this app runs in Amber Notes\n\n- It is the note: opening the note opens the app, on iPhone (320-440 pt wide, safe areas, the keyboard shrinks the view) and on the Mac (a window from about 500 to 1,800 px, resized live). Light and dark follow the device.\n- A normal Vite + React 19 + TypeScript + Tailwind 4 + shadcn/ui project. Amber Notes compiles it when a file is saved and says what broke; nothing is installed. React runs on preact/compat.\n- Data is JSON that Amber Notes keeps for the app: useStore(key, initial), useCollection(name), useSettings(defaults), batch(fn), setSummary(text) from \"@/lib/amber\". The person's AI reads and edits it as data.json.\n- Recharts needs its chart parts as direct children (no fragments).\n","/index.html":"\n\n \n \n Lift\n \n \n
